Hyic Isogonals

There are 520 isogonal polychora with one or more of the hyic family symmetries (H4 = [5,3,3] and the groups around it) listed below, by the symmetry under which each is isogonal and no larger one.

Every entry is written from the polychoron itself at the seed given (β = distances to the four H4 mirrors; for ixitic and ixoic, on the sheet named): cells by orbit, vertices with the dualcell's face count, edges by how many faces meet there, faces by shape. Names and full names are from the accepted list; the 449 shapes marked new or with a suggested name were found by the atlas and are not on the accepted lists; suggested names are built the way the lists build their own (ixoic_suggest.py).

Hyics 15 Kihyics 1 Ixitics 50 Ixoics 454

Hyics

Hyic symmetry [5,3,3] order 14400. This is the symmetry of the 120-cell and is quite common in the uniform polychora. This symmetry has 120 points of doic symmetry, 600 points of tettic symmetry, 720 points of pippic symmetry, and 1200 points of trippic symmetry. It has 72 rings with decagonal symmetry that generate 1440 spots with pentagon symmetry, 200 rings with hexagonal symmetry that generate 2400 trigonic regions, and 450 rings with square symmetry that create 3600 rectic areas. There are also 60 spheres with doic symmetry that create 7200 dyadic regions. White space creates 14400 points with no symmetry. As a reflection group with no diagram symmetry, each face of its chamber is one shape: the fifteen Wythoffian polychora of the 120-cell family below.

Ex

(Hexecosachoron). It has 600 regular tets. It has 120 vertices with a 12 faced dualcell. It has 720 pentagonic edges. Its faces are 1200 equits. It is isogonal under hyic symmetry with no variations. It is also isogonal under kihyic, ixitic, 5-swirldoic, 5-skidoic, 3-swirldoic, 3-skidoic, 2-swirldoic, 2-skidoic, 1-swirldoic, 1-skidoic, 5-spyritic and 5-skitettic symmetries.

β = (0.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000, 1.000000) · 600 cells · point

β = (1.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000, 0.000000) · 120 cells · point

Kihyics

Kihyic symmetry [5,3,3]⁺ order 7200. This is the chiral 120-cell symmetry. It has 120 points of kidoic symmetry, 600 points of kitettic symmetry, 720 points of kipippic symmetry, and 1200 points of kitrippic symmetry. It has 72 rings with decagonal symmetry that create 1440 kipeggic regions, 200 hexagonal rings that generate 2400 kitriggic regions, and 450 square rings that create 3600 essic regions. White space creates 7200 spots with no symmetry. The map of its chamber is one region: snixhi.

Snixhi

(Snub 600-120). It has 600 snits, 720 pegyps, 120 snids, 1200 trigypos, and 7200 irregular tets. It has 7200 vertices with a 9 faced dualcell. It has 7200+7200+7200 trigonic and 3600+3600+3600 tetragonic edges. Its faces are 2400+2400 equits, 1440 pentagons, and four types of 7200 scalene triangles. It is isogonal under kihyic symmetry with three degrees of variation.

β = (0.041816, 0.042090, 0.042301, 0.873793) · 9840 cells · region

Ixitics

Ixitic symmetry 5[3⁺,4,3⁺] order 1440. It has two sets of 120 points with kitettic symmetry. It has 80 rings with kihiggic symmetry which generate 480 kitriggic areas and 90 rings with square symmetry that generate 720 essic areas. White space generates 1440 no sym areas. It has index 10 in hyic, so its domain is ten copies of the hyic chamber (ten sheets), each mapped by curved walls; the mirrors are seams between sheets. Below: its regions (three degrees of variation), walls (two), lines (one) and tehi.

Tehi

(Tetrahedral 120). It has 120+480 regular tets. It has 120 vertices with a 12 faced dualcell. It has 720 pentagonic edges. Its faces are 480+720 equits. It is isogonal under ixitic symmetry with no variations. It is related to ex. Note: the 600-cell (ex) seen under ixitic: its vertex set has ex's full symmetry (14400).

Ixoics

Ixoic symmetry 5[[3⁺,4,3⁺]] order 2880. It has 240 points with kitettic symmetry, two sets of 480 points with kitrippic symmetry, and 720 points with kibrick symmetry. It has 80 hexagonal rings which generate 960 kitriggic areas and 90 octagonal rings that generate 1440 essic areas. White space creates 2880 no sym areas. It is not inside hyic (its elements need √2), so it has no exact wall forms over the golden field: each of its ten sheets was mapped numerically, and the walls and lines between the map's shapes were then searched for where the walls meet. Below: its regions (three degrees of variation), walls (two), lines (one) and points.

Bittid

(Bitetrahedral 240). It has 720 disphenoids and 1440 irregular tets. It has 240 vertices with a 20 faced dualcell. It has 1440 trigonic, 480 hexagonic, and 480 dodecagonic edges. Its faces are 1440 isots and 2880 scalene triangles. It is isogonal under ixoic symmetry with no variations. It is also isogonal under 4-swirldoic, 4-skidoic, 3-swirldoic, 2-swirldoic, 1-swirldoic, 5-scubic and 5-stettic symmetries.

Bitutid

(Bitruncatotetrahedral 240). It has 480 trigypos, 480 ratets, and 240 tuts. It has 1440 vertices with a 9 faced dualcell. It has 1440+2880 trigonic and 720+1440 tetragonic edges. Its faces are 960 equits, 960 ditrigons, 1440 isots, and 2880 scalene triangles. It is isogonal under ixoic symmetry with no variations. The atlas recorded it as a line, but no curve through its seed keeps the shape: no pair of the map's surfaces through it keeps the polytope a step of 1e-5 either way, and along the curve they share it changes within 1e-7 both ways, so it is a point (ixoic_line_equations.json). On sheet 7 it lies where two mirrors meet (b1 = b4 = 0): along that edge, b = (0, s, 1 − s, 0), are sagypbittid (0 ≤ s < √2 − 1), the point bamtuted (s = √2 − 1), masbittid (to s = 1/φ), bitutid (s = 1/φ), gasbittid (1/φ < s < 1) and bitutid again at the corner (0, 1, 0, 0).
